Structural cue: later the purified person seeks face/proximity; here the denier turns from it. Mechanism: the root holds the missed possibility of closeness. Abductive move: I infer that تولى rejects available ولاء before becoming withdrawal. Reading change: “turned away” also means he forfeited nearness and alliance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001684/B001","root_001684/B004","root_001684/B006","root_001684/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_375ffe53e1f29a577a34","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:16:3\",\"root_001684\",[\"B001\",\"B004\",\"B006\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Daha sonra Rabbin yüzünü arayan karşıt yöneliş, buradaki dönüşü erişilebilir yakınlığı, desteği ve yüz yüze yönü bilerek terk etmek olarak belirginleştirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001684/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_59d9660e5ee0233cd29f","text":"3. **Succession into the bad path.** Evidence: `و ل ي` B002 (continuous succession), activated by 92:10 facilitation and 92:15 following/fire contact. Structural cue: actions follow one another: deny, turn, meet fire. Mechanism: تولي is a sequence-maker, not just a single gesture. Abductive move: I infer moral momentum from successive order. Reading change: the ayah becomes the hinge where denial begins a chain.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001290/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5f04515548ddbd0e3f19","text":"1. **Denial plus withdrawal.** Evidence: `ك ذ ب` B002 (declaring false/denying), `و ل ي` B007 (turning away/withdrawal). Structural cue: the ayah identifies the “most wretched” by the same denial of 92:9 plus an added bodily turn. Mechanism: inward denial becomes spatial disengagement from guidance. Abductive move: I infer that refusal is completed when attention and face turn away. Reading change: the culprit is defined by direction, not only proposition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001684/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8604f2a5e3ba72dff425","text":"92:18 supplied the enacted opposite to failed confirmation, but 92:20 supplied the decisive surprise: `وَجْهِ` gives the missing positive vector and activates `و ل ي:B006` beside the direct withdrawal branch `B007`. Turning away is therefore defined by a relation one refuses to face.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001684/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8dfa4876cf7070bda4ea","text":"2. **Turning away defined by the direction not faced.** `تَوَلَّىٰ` directly anchors `و ل ي:B007` (turning away and withdrawal), but its root also holds `و ل ي:B006` (turning toward and facing) and `و ل ي:B001` (near adjacency). The later `وَجْهِ رَبِّهِ` in 92:20 supplies an explicit positive orientation, while 92:17 supplies protective removal for the opposed agent. The packet gives two vectors; the abductive move is that turning away is a deliberate loss of proximity to the face/direction that the other course seeks. `تَوَلَّىٰ` therefore becomes relational reorientation, not motion without an object.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001684/B002","root_001684/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_900ae0e37d6e52cacae1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:16:3\",\"root_001684\",[\"B002\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ardışıklık boyutu, yalanlama ile dönüşü tek bir jestten çok, kişiyi ateş karşılaşmasına taşıyan bir davranış zincirinin halkası hâline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c26d97a2e0554f7f7c4a","text":"92:18-19 strongly altered this reading: “صدق بالحسنى” became visible as charity-like truth, because the later giver offers wealth without repayment. The fall in 92:11 and warning in 92:14 precede; the giver who purifies himself in 92:18 supplies the contrary enacted course.
